title: D8 row-invariant draft verification repair
tool: Native quantization kernel and generation checks

The first VQ2.1/dense4 speculative generation gate failed and correctly stopped the task screen. Plain and depth-one greedy generation agreed; depth two and four changed the target token stream. Sampled state, callback stop and later EOS also failed. The failure and complete raw observations remain preserved below.

The existing D8 projection selected SIMD reduction at at most twenty routed pairs and scalar reduction above that threshold. This explains why three- and five-token verification did not retain one-token arithmetic. The focused prospective repair keeps the SIMD reduction across at most fifty routed pairs only in the existing row-invariant arithmetic mode. Original reference dispatch and ordinary prefill retain their prior behavior, and storage partitions continue using the whole operation's pair count. No golden or tolerance changes.

The nonconstant component checks pass for one, two, three and five token rows against concatenated one-token results. The identical full generation/state/cancellation gate then passes all assertions on the repaired source-bound binary. Memory stays inside the existing ten-GB process bound. The sixteen completed tasks follow as a separate operation; this capture does not claim their outcome, throughput, broad quality equivalence or product admission.
